## Implementation

Every supplied v2 proof is provisional until the same invocation renews the live approved origin and fresh graph. Every gate renews even with unchanged objects; previously advertised ref/OID pairs must still exist exactly. A final live advertisement follows fresh repository/approval binding, and the next Ready/effect shares a five-second monotonic window. Each volume effect repeats the gate. An origin/ref moved after the final observation cannot be detected atomically across hosts; this residual window is at most five seconds. No later effect reuses it. Canonical salvage name==OID, commit type, protected roots, v1/main behavior, header v2 and receipt schemas are retained.

The actual inventory observation is clock-bound to the same kernel boot/time namespace and call interval. This host has neither time-namespace interface and exercises the explicit global domain. A present but unreadable interface refuses. Native present-interface production execution remains owed; no production bypass was added. Ready input preparation precedes the final observation, without publishing Ready. Late-withdrawal tests assert receipt.json is absent. Custody predicates are retained while avoiding external dirname and repeated sed processes. Stale-window diagnostics report actual elapsed_ms.

Real temporary Git repositories, fake bare origins, actual writer/scanner/coordinator and an independent parent-DAG plus Git ancestry oracle are used. Four seeds 1901–1904 provide 280 independently expected shapes across eight exact methods. Their execution counts and current completion are listed below; do not infer completion from their implementation. No deliberate production mutants ran.

## Scope, costs and unlisted work

Final impacted-classes range selects linux-only-policy, nightly-live-lane, remote-scripts, spawn-limit-rosters, test-lane-category and timing-labels. Hand-glob class obligations are covered by declared rows; broad reverse-graph database/deployment/GC suggestions are recorded rather than silently expanded to an assembly. Source changes after the last saved range require read-only refresh. The whole Unit lane remains nightly/rc.

Unlisted work is prerequisite driver build/regeneration, read-only policy/digest computation, shell/PowerShell syntax checks, evidence/history/OS/impact lints, baseline attribution for the pre-existing label failure, and small clock/custody diagnostic probes. Every build/test driver was separately slot-gated; all were foreground and joined. Probes do not replace TRX rows. No proof repetitions are required after green; changed production/fixture inputs justify targeted repairs. No unsupported Review flake exception or timeout enlargement was used.

Build admission is separate from held execution. /proc/loadavg is sampled with run/row observations in host-samples.jsonl and row end records in results.jsonl; sampling is not continuous and not p95. Tool preparation/provenance can exceed compilation time because its ledger hashes prior ignored cold outputs. Task-owned stopped cold outputs were moved with literal absolute paths into .antiphon/evidence/card1901-round2/cold-build-archive; cold-build-archive-map.json preserves every original path. Same bytes retained, no source/TRX/report/native incremental output removed. Preparation then fell from about6min to9s. This is a performance observation, not a test verdict. Selections over30min need caller-owned performance Debug; Mutation performance work remains additional and unbounded by these sparse samples.
